## Method

- Active link checks target `selectedImageUrl`, not fallback candidates.
- Visual flags use a lower-resolution rendition of the same selected ESA asset where possible.
- Replacement candidates, when available, are other healthy Hubble Catalog 0019 records matching identity or sky position.
- FOV/orientation flags identify records requiring review; they do not silently rewrite values.
